115 年 10 月海關進出口貿易初步統計 
一、進出口貿易變動 
（一）出口 
10 月出口 967.6 億美元，創歷年單月新高，較上月增 10.9%，較上
年同月增 58.9%(按新臺幣計算增 66.6%)；累 計1 至 10 月出口 7,582.9
億美元，較上年同期增 47.7%(按新臺幣計算增 51.1%)。 
（二）進口 
10 月進口 688.9 億美元，亦創歷年單月新高，較上月增 8.3%，較上
年同月增 75.1%(按新臺幣計算增 83.5%)；累 計1 至 10 月進口 5,697.6
億美元，較上年同期增 44.6%(按新臺幣計算增 47.6%)。 
（三）出入超 
10 月出超 278.8 億美元，為單月新高，較上年同月增 63.3 億美元；
累計 1 至 10 月出超 1,885.4 億美元，較上年同期增 689.7 億美元。

二、出口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
受惠於人工智慧(AI)、 高效能運算及雲端服務等應用商機持續拓展，
加上適逢傳統外銷旺季，以及價格攀升效應，10 月出口較上年同月
增 358.7 億美元(+58.9%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 資通與視聽產品出口 476.0 億美元(歷年單月新高)， 較上年同月增
200.7 億美元(+72.9%)，其中電腦及其附屬單元增 151.6 億美元
(+67.5%)、 電腦之零附件增18.5 億美元(+1.0 倍)、 儲存媒體增17.3
億美元(+2.1 倍)、交換器及路由器增 11.7 億美元(+1.3 倍)。 
2. 電子零組件出口 326.8 億美元(歷年單月新高)， 較上年同月增124.3
億美元(+61.4%)，其中積體電路增 120.5 億美元(+63.0%)、印刷電
路增 1.9 億美元(+38.5%)、 電容器及電阻器增1.2 億美元(+48.6%)。 
3. 基本金屬及其製品出口 28.8 億美元，較上年同月增 7.6 億美元
(+35.7%)，其中銅及其製品增 4.7 億美元(+1.2 倍)、鋼鐵及其製品
增 1.9 億美元(+16.4%)。 
4. 機械出口 28.8 億美元(歷年單月新高)，較上年同月增 7.5 億美元
(+35.5%)，其中生產半導體等機械增 2.9 億美元(+57.5%)、機器、
工廠或實驗室設備增 1.4 億美元(+70.0%)。 
5. 礦產品出口 15.5 億美元，較上年同月增 6.5 億美元(+71.6%)，其
中石油煉製品增 5.7 億美元(+72.2%)。

（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 10 月出口較上年同期增 2,448.3 億美元(+47.7%)，以資通
與視聽產品增 1,423.3 億美元(+72.5%)、電子零組件增 850.5 億美元
(+47.5%)增加較多，礦產品、機械亦各增 41.9 億美元(+36.8%)、41.5
億美元(+19.8%)。

生產等出現結構性轉變，爰另予併計。

三、進口主要貨品 
（一）與上年同月比較 
隨 AI 產業鏈國際分工與出口衍生需求之帶動，積體電路、資通產品
及半導體設備進口持續強勁，加上國際原物料行情居高，10 月總進
口較上年同月增 295.5 億美元(+75.1%)，主要變動如下： 
1. 農工原料進口458.3 億美元， 較上年同月增195.0億美元(+74.0%)，
其中電子零組件增 136.1 億美元(+1.1 倍)、礦產品增 22.8 億美元
(+58.0%)、電腦之零附件增 16.2 億美元(+70.0%)、基本金屬增 6.2
億美元(+33.4%)、塑化原料增 5.0 億美元(+22.0%)。 
2. 資本設備進口 163.4 億美元， 較上年同月增 84.1 億美元(+1.1 倍)，
其中資通設備增 46.9 億美元(+2.0 倍)、半導體設備增 22.4 億美元
(+83.4%)、交通運輸設備增 7.6 億美元(+7.0 倍)。 
3. 消費品進口 61.1 億美元，較上年同月增 18.8 億美元(+44.5%)，其
中固態非揮發性儲存裝置增 16.0 億美元(+5.3 倍)、食品增 0.5 億
美元(+5.1%)、小客車增 0.4 億美元(+10.2%)。

四、主要出口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
10 月對美國、歐洲、日本出口創新高 
1. 對美國出口 329.3 億美元，較上年同月增 118.1 億美元(+55.9%)，
其中資通與視聽產品增 98.3 億美元(+56.6%)、電子零組件增 13.5
億美元(+1.7 倍)、機械增 2.4 億美元(+41.6%)。 
2. 對中國大陸與香港出口 204.4 億美元，較上年同月增 61.4 億美元
(+42.9%)，其中電子零組件增 47.4 億美元(+48.0%)、資通與視聽
產品增 5.6 億美元(+36.5%) 、基本金屬及其製品增 3.2 億美元
(+95.2%)。 
3. 對東協出口 184.6 億美元，較上年同月增 84.1 億美元(+83.8%)，
其中電子零組件增 38.6 億美元(+75.5%)、資通與視聽產品增 36.5
億美元(+1.4 倍)、礦產品增 5.0 億美元(+2.4 倍)。 
4. 對歐洲出口 59.5 億美元，較上年同月增 18.7 億美元(+46.0%)，其
中資通與視聽產品增 10.7 億美元(+47.9%)、電子零組件增 3.3 億
美元(+81.3%)、機械增 1.2 億美元(+50.8%)。 
5. 對日本出口 36.7 億美元，較上年同月增 12.2 億美元(+49.6%)，其
中電子零組件增 7.5 億美元(+63.2%)、資通與視聽產品增 3.2 億美
元(+1.3 倍)、化學品增 0.5 億美元(+47.0%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計 1 至 10 月對美國出口 2,407.1 億美元 ， 較上年同期增891.1 億美
元(+58.8%)，對中國大陸與香港出口 1,805.3 億美元，增 416.6 億美
元(+30.0%)， 對東協出口1,524.1 億美元 ， 增547.6 億美元(+56.1%)，
對歐洲出口 481.3 億美元 ， 增157.1 億美元(+48.5%)， 對日本出口314.4
億美元，增 70.5 億美元(+28.9%)。

五、主要進口國家/地區 
（一）與上年同月比較 
10 月自東協進口 134.1 億美元 ，較上年同月增 82.3 億美元(+1.6 倍)，
自南韓進口 130.0 億 美 元，增73.1 億美元(+1.3 倍)， 自中國大陸與香
港進口 128.3 億美元，增 51.9 億美元(+67.9%)，自日本進口 62.0 億
美元，增 18.8 億美元(+43.4%)，自美國進口 53.6 億美元，增 14.7 億
美元(+37.9%)，自歐洲進口 48.2 億美元，增 9.5 億美元(+24.6%)。 
（二）與上年同期比較 
累計1 至10 月自主要國家/地區進口與上年同期比較 ， 自東協增85.6%、
自南韓增 72.7%、自中國大陸與香港增 45.3%、自日本增 27.4%、自
美國增 23.0%、自歐洲增 12.1%。

六、主要國家/地區貿易概況 
（一）今年 1-9 月主要國家/地區出口均呈上升，其中南韓(1-10 月)、香港、
新加坡受 AI 相關需求帶動科技供應鏈及區域貿易活動熱絡，累計出
口分別年增 60.6%、52.3%、41.3%；中國大陸、美國各年增 21.3%
及 14.3%，德國(1-8 月)、日本各增 8.8%、8.3%。 
（二）主要國家/地區進口同步成長，累計 1-9 月以香港進口年增 51.8%較
高，新加坡、 中國大陸、南 韓(1-10 月)分別年增 39.0%、27.5%及 21.4%，
德國(1-8 月)、日本、美國各增 8.4%、8.2%、6.7%。

### Page 7

7
圖 5 2026 年主要國家/地區出進口年增率
七、綜合分析
（一）隨全球人工智慧(AI)基礎建設投資強勁成長，帶動相關供應鏈需求擴
增，加以外銷產品價格攀升，10 月出口達 967.6 億美元，首度突破
900 億美元，年增 58.9%，連續 36 個月正成長；進口688.9 億美元，
同為歷年單月最高，年增 75.1%，主要受惠於 AI 產業之國際分工與
出口引申需求增加，以及國際原物料行情高漲；出超 278.8 億美元亦
創新高。累計 1 至 10 月出口年增 47.7%、進口增 44.6%。
（二）10 月出口主要貨品中，資通與視聽產品、電子零組件出口值雙創單月
新高，各年增 72.9%及 61.4%，主因 AI、高效能運算及雲端服務等應
用商機熱絡；其餘貨類出口平均增 25.7%，其中礦產品因油價支撐增
71.6%，機械、電機產品受惠於半導體業者擴充產能及AI 需求外溢，
增幅皆達 3 成， 基本金屬及其製品亦增35.7%。累 計1 至 10 月資通與
視聽產品、電子零組件合占總出口比重約 8 成，年增 60.6%。

（三）10 月對主要市場出口年增率均逾 4 成，以對東協增 83.8%較突出，
對美國、日本、歐洲出口規模皆為單月新高，各增 55.9%、49.6%及
46.0%， 對中國大陸與香港增42.9%。累 計1 至 10 月對五大市場出口
增幅介於 28%~59%， 其中對美國出口年增 58.8%、對東協增 56.1%，
占總出口比重各為 31.7%及 20.1%，分別寫下近 36 年同期及歷年同
期新高紀錄。
（四）展望未來，全球經濟仍面臨地緣政治 衝突、美國貿易政策走向等不
確定因素，惟隨人工智慧應用逐漸擴散至推論、代理式 AI，且國際
雲端服務業者持續布建 AI 基礎設施，帶動半導體、伺服器等相關供
應鏈需求延續，加以科技新品相繼推 出、年底消費旺季來臨之挹注，
我國第 4 季出口可望維持強勁成長態勢。

### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 澳洲 8 月就業人數勁增 3.95 萬人大幅優於預期，但勞動參與率上升至 67.1%，
推升失業率至 4.6%，呈現勞動力擴增但市場邊際降溫跡象。澳洲央行總裁布拉
克日前指出，失業率回升至5%有助於抑制頑固通膨。考量物價長期高於目標中
點，市場預期澳洲央行本週二將重啟緊縮，升息 1 碼至 4.60%，創 2011年底以
來新高。
 瑞士央行（SNB）上週四宣布連續第五次將關鍵政策利率維持在0%不變，符合
市場預期。儘管受油價推升使 8 月通膨微升至 0.8%，但仍處於目標區間，央行
預期中期通膨壓力溫和，現行極度寬鬆立場依然適當。決議公布後瑞郎兌美元跌
至 1.2077。相較全球主要央行相繼緊縮，瑞士央行維持超低利率，策略轉為視情
況調節匯市以穩定瑞郎。
 標普全球公布歐元區 9 月綜合 PMI 初值升至 53.1，創三年新高，製造與服務業
同步擴張，新訂單增速達四年多最快，印證經濟無懼美伊衝突引發的能源成本飆

升，展現超預期韌性。受能源價格傳導加速影響，通膨壓力再度升溫，儘管尚未
浮現薪資二輪效應，但強勁景氣數據促使市場大幅提高歐洲央行提前於10月例
會再度升息的押注。
 美國 9 月綜合 PMI 初值勁升至 58.4，創 62 個月新高；製造業與服務業 PMI 亦
逆勢走強至 57與 58.7，凸顯企業擴張強勁，引發市場對通膨持續性的擔憂。市
場焦點轉向 9 月 30日公布的美國 8 月 PCE、個人消費支出與 Q2 GDP終值，同
日日本亦將發布短觀調查與央行意見摘要。若終端消費延續強勁，將牽動美日央
行政策利率預期。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.655，低點在 31.866，以 31.790作收，較前一周收盤價
升值 2.8分。 
上週台幣先升後貶，周初，台股創新高，外資連續五日買超台股並匯入，推升
台幣最高來到 31.655，不過逢低吸引進口商、政府基金等買盤進場，台幣主要在
31.700 附近震盪；臨近周末，美國 9 月綜合 PMI 由 8 月的 56 走升至 58.4，創下逾
5 年來的新高，加上美伊戰事持續，皆推升市場對後續升息的預期，美元指數升至
101 上方，台幣跳空開低，回貶至 31.800 上方，最低來到 31.866，不過隨後出口商
賣壓出籠，台幣跌勢放緩，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.790。 
＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國經濟表現仍具韌性，9月綜合
PMI 由 8 月的 56 上升至 58.4，企業活動加速至逾 5 年來最強，數據公布後美國 10
年債券殖利率升破 5%，Fed Watch 顯示市場對於聯準會 10 月升息預期升高，有助
於美元指數維持偏強格局，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，台股在創下
新高後回落，而外資也結束連續五日買超的走勢，轉為賣超，短線上資金匯入不利
台幣表現，然而，台灣8 月外銷訂單達 1,029.6億美元、年增 71.4%，創歷史新高，
AI 需求帶動出口持續強勁，出口商後續拋匯需求預計將提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，
預估本週台幣區間震盪。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Sep/29/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走貶。人民幣中間價在上周連續十天走升，並升破6.7500整數
關口，最高來到 6.7468，帶動人民幣偏升，不過由於美伊戰事持續，市場對於聯準
會升息預期提升，美元指數震盪走強，加上週間公布美國9月綜合 PMI由 8 月的 56
走升至 58.4，創下逾 5 年來的新高，美元指數突破 101 整數關卡，令境外人民幣承
壓，USD/CNH回貶至 6.7200上方，終場境外人民幣收在6.7227，週線貶值283點，
境內人民幣則收在 6.7125，週線貶值 141 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣高檔震盪。就國際市場來看，美中兩國完成新一輪談判，美方
表示雙方同意將原訂 11 月 10 日到期的貿易休戰延長至明年 1 月 10 日，並持續就
關稅、貿易及供應鏈等議題進行協商，降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險，對人民幣形
成一定支撐。不過，美國近期經濟數據仍具韌性，加以聯準會偏鷹立場推升美元，
預計將限制人民幣升值空間；就中國國內市場來看，近期人行持續偏強設定中間價，
帶動人民幣維持偏強走勢，惟美中利差再度擴大，預計將限制人民幣升值的空間；
最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH日線圖中，匯價反彈至 20日均線之上，顯示原先下
行動能暫歇，上方壓力先看向季線 6.7350附近，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣高檔震
盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6900-6.7400

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY先上後下。週初，美國多位聯準會官員鷹派談話，帶動美元指數
持續上行，推升 USD/JPY延續前一週的走勢來到 157上方；週間，受美國 PMI 強
於預期與美債殖利率全面上行帶動，市場重新提高對聯準會後續升息路徑的押注，
帶動 USD/JPY大漲突破 159的關卡；臨近週末，日本首相高市早苗表態日圓被低估
是個問題，使市場提高對政策訊號與潛在干預的敏感度，帶動USD/JPY回吐大部分
漲幅，終場收在 157.26，週線上漲 56點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本財務大臣片山皋月上週
五罕見透露，美國總統川普在美日元首峰會上對日圓過度疲弱表達關切，首相高市
早苗亦回應日圓遭低估確屬問題。儘管近期美元指數維持在 101 上方，但在日本政
府可能干預的陰影下，預計將限縮日圓貶值的空間。二、美國9 月綜合 PMI產出指
數創逾五年新高，疊加美伊戰事推升油價突破100美元與柴油大漲，通膨壓力劇增。
美聯儲理事巴爾表態可能需再升息兩次，芝加哥聯儲古爾斯比也示警能源衝擊具持
續性，推升市場押注 10 月升息機率升至六成，預計將支撐 USD/JPY 走勢。三、就
技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY連日上漲一度站回年線但隨即失守，目前依舊承
壓於季線及年線下方，不過各天期均線趨平，且下方尚有月線支撐，短線尚無明確
的方向。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日幣：155.30-159.30

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Sep/29/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，歐元延續前一週偏弱的走勢，加上聯準會官員鷹
派發言，導致歐元跌破 1.1450的重要關卡；週間，儘管油價自高檔回落，但市場解
讀為能源成本仍處高位、難以迅速回落，推升對美國實質利率與利差優勢的預期。
加上歐洲在能源依賴與成長動能較弱下，相對於美國更難承受長期高利率環境，歐
元最低來到 1.1358；臨近週末，歐元跌深反彈，終場收在1.1391，週線下跌 91點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐洲央行首席經濟學家連
恩示警，第二波能源價格飆升將推升通膨，預計至2027年中期始回落至目標水準。
德國央行月報指出，受中東衝突、低天然氣庫存及明年醫療改革影響，通膨將居高
不下；夏季雖因出口疲弱與乾旱致增長趨緩，但在製造業回暖與財政支出挹注下，
估年底經濟有望重啟復甦，預計將提供歐元一定的支撐。二、美聯儲官員接連釋放
強烈鷹派信號，聖路易聯儲總裁穆薩萊姆直言當前 3.75%-4.00%利率仍偏寬鬆，呼
籲盡早收緊；芝加哥聯儲總裁古爾斯比亦警告，AI投資繁榮引發需求過熱，疊加持
續性供應衝擊，可能迫使美聯儲加速且更積極地升息。目前Fedwatch顯示預期明年
4 月前可能再升息三次，將不利歐元表現。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD
在各天期均線下方，且接連失守1.15及 1.14整數關卡，空方動能較佔優勢。綜合以
上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1300-1.1450

                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.09.21-115.09.24 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.804% ~ 0.830%  
30天期短票利率   1.65% ~ 1.69%  
公債－10 年期-A15109     1.95%  
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.84%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本周央行持續每日減發定存單，令市場資金充裕，帶動短
率靠向區間低位。 
週二央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元，加權平均得標利率為 1.638%，略低於
市場預期，且較上次標售結果下跌 2.5個基本點。 
週四隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.804%~0.830%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.65%~1.69%，利率較上週下跌 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109 及 5 年期指標券 A15108 均未成
交，週四等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.95%、1.84%。 
台股排擠效應、國際債市走勢偏空，加上油價續處高檔，通膨潛在威脅仍存，
12 月升息可能性未能完全排除，市場氣氛續偏觀望，預期台債殖利率將維持
高檔區間盤整 ， 預估5年券區間為 1.84%~1.90%，10年券區間為 1.95%~2.0%。

2026 年三季度货币政策执
行报告学习体会

货币政策趋稳   
2026 年三季度货币政策执行报告内容改动之处不多，我们认为，一定程度上意
味着货币政策中长期目标“建设金融强国”成为未来一段时间内的主要任务。 
 2026 年三季度货币政策执行报告中，相比二季度执行报告改动的内容不多： 
 一是对世界经济的判断，从“动能疲弱”变为“韧性不足”，以及出现了“主要经济
体经济表现分化” ， 对于通胀和货币政策 ， 二季度中提到的“全球通胀水平有所抬升，
主要经济体央行货币政策趋向调整”，也调整成了三季度的“通胀走势和货币政策调
整存在不确定性” 。 我们认为主要原因在于两方面 ， 一是2026 年带动全球经济增长的
人工智能科技发展，随着美国中期选举结果落定，开始遭受监管压力，二是推升2026
年通胀的国际能源价格，也有可能随着美国对外战略的调整，结束供给紧张的格局。
但是从全球范围内来看，一方面通胀有效回落需要时间，另一方面经济增长动力切换
不太容易，因此不排除在特定时间内，全球经济增长韧性面临“通胀处于高位、经济
增速下行、货币政策收紧”的三重考验。 
 二是在国内经济形势判断的部分，二季度的判断是“经济长期向好的内在支撑更加坚
实”，三季度则提出“仍面临供强需弱、结构分化、外部冲击等问题和挑战”。我们
认为这一提法对应的是我国经济增长表现出的两个特点，一是从内部来看，与全球经
济增长类似的 K 型分化，以电子行业为代表的人工智能产业链，对我国进出口、工业
增加值、工业企业利润数据都形成较明显的拉动，二是从外部影响看，2026 年前三个
季度三驾马车中净出口的累计同比贡献率逐步上升。因此无论是从产业链角度，或全
球化角度，我国经济都更容易受全球经济增长的影响。 
 三是关于增量政策和政策协同，二季度货币政策执行报告中提到的是“充分发挥各项
存量政策效能 ， 及时谋划出台务实管用的增量政策” ， “加强与财政政策协同配合”，
“发挥好货币政策工具总量和结构双重功能”，三季度则是“发挥存量政策与增量政
策的集成效应”和“发挥好各类结构性货币政策工具作用”，三季度货币政策执行报
告中，增加了“‘六张网’建设”作为金融支持的重点领域之一。我们认为该部分内
容的调整，主要指向的是货币政策的“效能”而非一直以来货币政策宽松指向的“增
量”，可能较大程度上会降低市场对于货币政策持续宽松的预期。 
 四是货币政策改革更加具体，二季度货币政策执行报告中提到“推动贷款定价基准多
元化”，在三季度货币政策执行报告中已经具体为“推动贷款利率定价基准从比较单
一地参考 LPR 定价向更加多元的定价基准转型” ， 并且“目前已有参考 DR001 定价
的贷款陆续落地”。我们认为现阶段货币政策改革的目标不仅是降低实体经济融资成
本，同时也是加强货币政策传导的改革。 
 五是专栏的部分依旧是五个 ， 我们比较关注的是“专栏 3 设立债市非银大额赎回互换
便利”。“专栏 2 国际利率水平比较”也有提到，我国当前利率、利差，无论是与历
史水平相比还是与海外相比，都处于偏低水平，因此货币政策工具创设和使用的一个
重要考虑，都是“为经济稳定增长、高质量发展和金融市场平稳运行营造适宜的货币
金融环境”。 
 我们认为下一阶段货币政策的重要任务是聚焦完成中长期目标，也就是“金融强国”
建设。 
 三季度我国货币政策大事包括 ：7 月 24 日 ， 中国人民银行联合科技部等8 部门印发 《关
于加强科技金融领域数据开发利用的通知》 ；8 月 14 日，中国人民银行、国家外汇管
理局联合发布《关于跨国公司本外币跨境资金集中运营业务有关事宜的通知》；8 月
28 日，中国人民银行会同金融监管总局印发《关于改革完善房地产信贷管理 推动加
快构建房地产发展新模式的意见》；9 月 28 日，中国人民银行联合金融监管总局、中
国证监会、国家发展改革委、工业和信息化部、财政部、商务部、文化和旅游部印发
《关于金融支持服务业扩能提质的指导意见》 ；9 月 28 日，中国人民银行印发《关于
抵押补充贷款政策加力支持“六张网”建设有关事宜的通知》，下调抵押补充贷款利率
0.25 个百分点至 1.50%，将“六张网”建设纳入抵押补充贷款支持领域；9 月 28 日，中
国人民银行办公厅印发《关于加大科技创新和技术改造再贷款政策支持力度有关事宜
的通知》 ，增加科技创新和技术改造再贷款额度2000 亿元，并将该项再贷款支持比例
从 60%提高至 100%；9 月 28 日，中国人民银行决定增加支农支小再贷款额度 5000
亿元 ， 其中民营企业再贷款额度增加 3000 亿元 ， 激励引导地方法人金融机构扩大对涉
农、小微和民营企业的信贷投放；9 月 29 日，财政部会同中国人民银行、金融监管总
局印发《关于实施居民购房贷款贴息政策的通知》。此外，还有同部分国家续签双边
本币互换协议，以及任命海外人民币业务清算行事宜。 
 我们认为下一阶段货币政策可能采取对内“稳”对外“进”的方针：对国内经济和金融市
场以稳定为主，重点在于营造适宜经济高质量发展和平稳运行的货币金融环境，货币
政策方面，增量政策工具可能减少，政策重点在于发挥存量政策效能和加大现有货币
政策应用；对国外以推进人民币国际化、打造“人民币-人民币资产”循环为主，保持人
民币汇率弹性，不断增强人民币和人民币资产对国际资本的吸引力。 
 风险提示：全球通胀二次上行；全球经济增长动能减弱；地缘政治局势发展前景的不
确定性。

突和经贸摩擦多发频发，供给冲击和输入型通胀压力影响仍在

评级体系说明 
以报告发布日后公司股价/行业指数涨跌幅相对同期相关市场指数的涨跌幅的表现为基准： 
公司投资评级： 
买    入：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 20%以上； 
增    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 10%-20%； 
中    性：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数变动幅度在-10%-10%之间； 
减    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数跌幅在 10%以上； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

行业投资评级： 
强于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现强于基准指数； 
中    性：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现基本与基准指数持平； 
弱于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现弱于基准指数； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

或恒生中国企业指数；美股市场基准指数为纳斯达 克综合指数或标普 500 指数。

### Page 3

Investment Thesis and Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment thesis - Hiwin Corp 
Hiwin is the second-largest global linear motion supplier with 18% market share in 2024. 
It continues to witness decelerating market share growth from 2020 when it started to 
pursue a deleveraging strategy.   
Although we expect Hiwin to continue to benefit from rising demand from the 
semiconductor/EV industry given the upgrading trend, we are Sell rated, given: (1) we 
expect a weak end-market demand recovery and high inventory levels for the near term, 
leading to high pricing pressure and a stagnant utilization rate; (2) continued 
overcapacity issues in mainland China, where the company has higher exposure (45% in 
2024) vs. peers such as THK (24%), and we expect this could lead to further 
deterioration in Hiwin’s revenue/profitability outlook in the near term; and (3) the 
negative impact from its long-term deleveraging strategy. Hiwin continues to pay back 
long-term debt and expand capacity to better compete with peers, which we believe will 
further impact EPS growth and valuation. The stock is currently trading above its 
historical average valuation (33.0x). We expect its P/E multiple to derate once the 
market begins to adjust for a prolonged downcycle and expect downgrades to 
consensus EPS growth/ROE estimates, suggesting high downside risk to Hiwin’s 
valuation.  
Price target risks and methodology - Hiwin Corp

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that

### Page 1

ab 10 November 2026
Global Research
All Ring Tech
Bright 2027 outlook with multiple drivers
Intact 2027 outlook
All Ring's 2027 outlook is robust, supported by three key drivers. 1) We expect TSMC
and ASE to continue accelerating CoWoS capacity expansion, with industry capacity
reaching ~270kwpm by end-2027E, up from ~160kwpm by end-2026. We estimate
TSMC and ASE’s capacity could reach ~180kwpm and ~70kwpm, respectively, by
2027E. 2) Second, with Intel's EMIB-T progressing on track toward volume production

by early 2028, we expect related equipment demand to begin ramping in 2027,
providing a new growth driver for All Ring. 3) CPO’s fiber array unit (FAU) coupling tools
start contributing to All Ring's 2027 revenue, with demand from Nvidia and AMD.
More concrete CPO development in 2027-28
Our industry feedback suggests Nvidia is taking the lead to aggressively push CPO mass
production in 2027 for scale up switch solution. AMD is also engaging with key

equipment suppliers, in preparation for competing CPO solution. Competition among
the equipment vendors is fierce, but we believe All Ring is well positioned in FAU
coupling as a tier 1 supplier, given the superior execution in driving higher tool
throughput versus peers. Considering the meaningful tool demand in 2027-28, we
forecast All Ring to recognize ~100 tools for revenue in 2027E, contributing ~12% of All
Ring's sales next year. Potential upside to our current estimates could come from larger

TAM for faster capacity expansion, All Ring securing higher share / ASP through better
tool performance.
Lower Q3 gross margin due to mix
Q3 EPS came in below LSEG consensus by -10% as gross margin of 48.5% missed
consensus of 54.2%. Lower gross margin could be due to a higher mix of automation
products, which carries a lower margin vs corporate average. For Q4, considering lighter
seasonality and current visibility, we model sales to decline by -28% from Q3 with gross

margin of 53% and EPS of NT$7.57.
Valuation: Maintain Buy and target of $1700
Post Q3 results, we fine-tune 2026 EPS by -4% due to lower gross margin in the quarter,
though our estimates for 2027/28 are largely kept unchanged. Our 2027E/2028E EPS
are 3%/30% ahead of Consensus pre-earnings. We maintain our Buy rating and price
target of $1700 based on 27x average 2027-28E PE.

Company Description
Founded in 1996, All Ring is a Taiwan-based supplier of automation equipment for the
semiconductor back-end packaging and passive component manufacturing industries. The
company's products include underfill dispensers, heatsink placement and automated optical
inspection (AOI) tools for advanced packaging equipment, particularly for heterogeneous

integration. Revenue is generated primarily from sales of semiconductor and electronic
manufacturing equipment and related automation solutions, with more than 90% of
revenue derived from Taiwan.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value All Ring based on PE methodology.
Risks for All Ring and the semis industry:
- Geopolitical uncertainties: Geopolitical uncertainties including tariffs and more export
control could negatively impact semis cycle, and a downturn would lead to slower demand in

the overall market and moderating capacity expansion.
- Meaningful deceleration of new technology development: Slower demand and less
investment are expected If there is meaningful deceleration of new technology development
such as Cloud AI, autonomous driving, etc.
- Rising utility cost: Larger-than-expected utility cost increases would further pressure
profitability. Rising utility costs in Taiwan have negatively impacted margins for Taiwan tech

companies.
Company risks:
- Faster- or slower-than-expected CoWoS capacity expansion: This could result in All Ring
gaining/losing equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs.
- Competitors achieving technological advancement: Potential risk of competition (from
either local or foreign companies) achieving technological advancements could result in All
Ring losing market share from major foundries and OSATs.
- Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth: Better- or worse-than-expected AI growth could

lead to a ramp-up or a slowdown of equipment orders from major foundries and OSATs to All
Ring, affecting long-term revenue growth potential.
- Slowdown or delay of new technological advancement: A potential slowdown or delay of
the rollout for technological advancements for technologies such as SoIC, CoPoS, and CPO,
would limit future equipment orders.
- Increase of raw material costs: Rising raw material costs may directly impact All Ring’s
margins for equipment shipments.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
                        MPI Corporation (6223.TWO)                    
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture 
the long-term value. We assume a 9.78% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium 
6.0%), a payout ratio of 67%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.0%, and a terminal growth rate 
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 50x/25x 2027e/2028e P/E. We believe its valuation will con-
tinue to climb along with growing earnings. 
                        Risks to Upside                    
n Stronger-than-expected AI demand
n Stronger-than-expected market share gain from peers
n Stronger-than-expected CPO development

Risks to Downside                    
n Slower-than-expected AI demand
n Slower-than-expected market share gain from peers
n Slower-than-expected CPO development

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

기업분석 2025.09.28 www.daishin.com

Issue & News 
SK하이닉스  
(000660) 
 소음보다 방향성에 
류형근

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 3,200,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.09.23) 
1,862,000 
반도체업종

◼ 최근 시장은 2가지 Risk를 제기. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 
◼ 현 주가는 보다 강인해지는 Fundamental의 가치를 미반영한 수준. 
◼ 비중 확대 전략 유효. 목표주가와 매수의견 유지.

투자전략: 업황을 대변하지 못하고 있는 주가 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 현 주가는 업황의 강세를 충분히 반영하지 못하
고 있다고 생각. 1) 메모리반도체 사이클의 추가 확장 (구매 경쟁 심화에 따른 
단기 가격 상승 폭 확대, 기대치를 상회할 4Q26 DRAM 판매량), 2) 3Q26 실
적발표에서 보다 구체화될 주주환원 정책의 방향성에 보다 집중할 필요. 
시장에서 제기하는 Risk가 있다면, 2가지. 1) 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈, 
2) Solidigm IPO와 해외 공장 투자에 따른 경계심리 확대가 그 대상. 당사는 
해당 Risk가 Fundamental을 저해할 것으로 보고 있지 않으며, 단기 노이즈보
다는 사이클 확장의 선순환에 보다 주목할 필요가 있다고 생각. 
1. HBM4 품질 이슈, 과도한 우려는 기우 
3Q26부터 HBM4 출하가 본격화될 것이라는 기존 입장을 유지. HBM4 비중
은 3Q26 전체 판매량의 40%, 2027년 연간 50% 중반까지 상승할 것. 
일각에서는 주요 고객사향 HBM4 품질 이슈로 사업 성과가 기대치를 하회할 
수 있다는 우려를 표출. 다만, 과도한 우려는 기우. 후공정 단에서 문제가 일
부 발생했을 수 있으나, 해결 불가능한 Risk라 보기 어렵고, 불량에 따른 배
상금을 논할 단계도 아니라는 판단. 
2. 해외 공장 투자, 실보다 득이 크다 
언론보도에 따르면, SK하이닉스는 해외 공장 투자와 Solidigm IPO를 추진 중. 
해당 결정이 현실화될 경우, 시장은 단기적으로 이를 부정적으로 받아들일 
가능성이 존재. 다만, 결과적으로, 실보다 득이 큰 선택이 될 것이라 생각. 
- 해외 투자가 현실화되더라도, 실질 생산 기여 시점은 2028년 이후일 가능
성이 높음. 미국의 건설 경기와 공장 구축의 세부 단계 등을 감안할 필요. 
- 해외 투자 결정이 관세 Risk를 축소시킬 수 있는 변화라는 점도 감안할 필
요. “불확실성 해소”는 Upcycle의 중반부에서 반도체 주식에 대한 좋은 투자
포인트가 되어왔고, 이번에도 다르지 않을 것이라는 판단.

### Page 1

www.imeritz.com

삼성전자 (005930)

Company Brief 
반도체/디스플레이 
Analyst 김선우 
sunwoo.kim@meritz.co.kr 
RA 우서현 
seohyun.woo@meritz.co.kr 2027년 메모리 판가·실적 상향 조정  
3Q26E 영업이익 106.1조원, 원화강세 불구 견조한 수준 기록 전망 
삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익은 106.1조원으로 낮아진 시장 기대치 (104조원)를 소폭 
능가할 전망이다. 해당 분기동안 급격한 원화강세로 인해 실적 측면의 비우호적 환경
이 조성되었지만, 동사는 강건한 판매 정책에 기반해 메모리 판가가 산업 평균을 재
차 능가하며 견조한 실적 개선이 가능했다. 
동사 메모리 사업부의 3Q26 영업이익은 109조원으로 예상되며, 이중 DRAM과 
NAND는 각각 80조원 (OPM 78.5%) 과 29조원 (OPM 68.9%) 을 차지할 전망이다. 
DRAM과 NAND 출하량은 기존 예상치를 소폭 하회하는 +3.4%/+4.2% QoQ을 기
록하겠지만, 판가가 무려 +20.1%/19.0% QoQ 상승하며 실적 개선을 이끌겠다. 전
분기에 이어 ‘DS 특별경영성과급’ 충당금이 반영되며 손익에 일부 영향을 미치겠지
만, 동사 특유의 ‘수익성 극대화’ 판가 정책이 산업 우위 구도를 조성한다. 한편 LSI/
파운드리는 영업손실이 1.5조원을 기록하며 적자가 지속될 전망이다. 가동률 개선에 
기반한 사업 회복이 진행중이지만, 테일러팹 가동과 함께 비용 요인이 지속 출회할 
전망이다. 한편 반도체 외 기타 사업부의 험난한 실적 추이는 지속될 전망이다. SDC 
1.1조원 영업이익을 제외하고, DX (세트) 부문의 경우 MX -1.9조원, DA/VD -7,400
억원으로 영업적자 확대되리라 예상된다. 당사가 지난해 말부터 주장했듯, 세트 사업
은 메모리 등 부품원가 비중이 급격히 상승하는 가운데 향후 ‘판가 인상 → 판매량 
축소’의 ‘내구재화’ 변모 속 적자 최소화 노력만이 불가피할 전망이다.

1Q27 일부 모바일 DRAM 판가 +50% 폭등 예상. 적극적 매수 권고 
AGI를 향한 경쟁적이며 구조적 서버 수요 증가 불구, 스마트폰 중심의 B2C 응용처
는 유일한 판가저항처로 남아있다. 하지만 당사의 채널체크에 따르면, 메모리 업체들
의 지속적인 생산 전환 (LPDDR → 서버 DRAM 및 HBM)에 기반해 주요 고객 향 
모바일 메모리 판가는 무려 1Q27부터 50% 이상 QoQ 상향될 전망이다. 주요 고객
은 내년 20주년 플래그십 스마트폰을 제작하는 상황에서 판가 폭등을 감내해야하는 
상황이다. 최근 메모리 사이클 개선 속도에 대한 막연하며 진부한 우려가 존재하나, 
향후 1~2개월간 메모리 업체들에 대한 2027년 실적 추정치 상향과 함께 ‘성장·저평
가·주주환원 매력’이 동시에 발생할 전망이다. 동사의 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 기
존의 562조원에서 632조원으로 13% 상향조정하며, 시장 눈높이 역시 지속 상향되
리라 예상한다. 한편 배당 중심으로 발표됐던 동사의 주주환원의 경우, 모든 주주에게 
더 실효적인 ‘자사주 매입·소각’ 방안이 병행되는 방향으로 변모하리라 예상된다. 투
자의견 Buy와 적정주가 50만원을 유지한다. 
 Meritz Research  2026. 10. 7

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 500,000원 
현재주가 (10.6) 272,000원 
상승여력 83.8%

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기 환율 부진에도 영업이익 100조는 넘긴다 
삼성전자 26년 3분기 실적은 이전 전망치를 하회할 전망이다. 매출액은 195.2조원으
로 이전 분기 대비 13.8% 증가하고, 영업이익은 101.7조원으로 13.6% 증가할 것으
로 예상한다. 영업이익률은 52.1%로 전 분기 대비 0.1%p 하락할 전망이다. 원/달러 
환율 영향이 주요 변수로 작용한 것으로 추정한다. 2분기에 집행되지 않았던 성과급 
일부가 3분기에 반영된 영향도 있을 것으로 예상한다. DS 부문은 매출액 149.9조원, 
영업이익은 101.8조원으로 추정하고, 디스플레이 매출액 8.8조원, 영업이익 0.9조
원, MX/네트워크는 매출액 34.3조원, 영업적자 1.1조원으로 예상한다. VD/가전도 
영업적자 규모가 커질 것으로 예상한다.  
주주환원은 주가 상승을 견인할 것 
8월 21일 이후 주주환원에 대한 관심이 커졌다. 자사주 규모는 크지 않겠지만 배당
을 통한 주주환원 규모는 사상 최대 규모일 것으로 예상한다. 글로벌 대표 IT업체와 
비교해도 손색없는 수준이다. 메모리 업체의 주주환원 규모가 100조원 넘게 집행된 
적이 없어서 주가에 어떠한 영향을 미칠 지가 관전 포인트인데 긍정적 역할을 할 것
으로 기대한다. 자사주를 통한 EPS 개선은 아니지만, 안정적이고 지속적인 규모의 
주주환원은 주가를 한층 더 높은 수준에서 평가 받는 계기가 될 것으로 판단한다.  
현저한 저평가 구간, 이전 전망 유지 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 메모리 공급 부족 상황이 장기화될 
것으로 전망하고, 이로 인해 수익성은 현재 수준에서 유지되고, 이익 규모는 안정적
으로 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 현저히 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 목
표주가는 460,000원을 유지한다.

### Page 4

삼성전자 (005930)

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
반도체 Check Point 
1. 차별화된 주주환원 전략. 배경은 안정적 이익 규모 
2026년에는 대대적인 규모의 주주환원이 예상된다. 100 조원 내외 수준으로 
예상하는데 상당 부분은 배당으로 집행될 것으로 추정한다. 배당성향이 25%가 유지될 
것으로 예상하기 때문이다. 자사주 매입 여력은 20조원 내외 수준일 것으로 예상한다. 
주주환원은 주가 밸류 상승에 기여할 것으로 기대한다. 일회성이 아니고 장기적으로 
높은 수준의 주주환원이 가능하다는 것은 싸이클 변동에 따른 이익의 변동성이 
낮다는 의미이기도 하다. 
2. AI 모델 개발 속도 조절. 그래도 메모리 수요를 줄일 수는 없다 
Anthropic CEO는 현재 AI 개발 속도가 지나치게 빨라서 인간의 능력을 뛰어 넘는 
임계점에 이를 가능성이 높아져서 개발 속도를 늦출 필요가 있다고 언급했다. 이를 
Meta, Google, SpaceX 수장들이 공감했다. 우선 이들의 공감으로 AI 모델의 개발 
속도가 둔화될 것으로 보고 있지 않다. 또한, 이를 가정하더라도 향후 AI 모델은 
Agent AI로 진화할 것으로 보고 있다. 이에 따른 메모리에 대한 필요성은 급격히 
증가할 것으로 기대한다. 
3. 자신감 넘치는 HBM  
HBM4가 2026년 하반기부터 주력 제품이 될 것으로 전망한다. 26 년 3분기에는 
비중이 40% 내외, 4 분기에는 60%를 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 연초 기대 대비로는 
낮은 수준이다. 삼성전자의 점유율만 보면 연초 대비 높은 수준의 점유율이 예상된다.  
HBM4 물량이 예상보다 늦게 본격화되면서 2026년 HBM 시장 규모는 이전 전망 
대비 축소될 전망이다. HBM4도 늦은 출시로 연내 생산 물량은 이전 전망치인 130억 
Gb을 하회할 것으로 예상한다.  
2027년 HBM4 가격이 최근에 확정이 되었고, 이를 통해서 DRAM 수준의 수익성 
확보가 가능할 것으로 기대한다. 수익성 발목을 잡았던 HBM은 2027년 물량부터는 
영업이익 개선의 변수가 될 것으로 전망한다.

장기적 주주환원은 
안정적 이익이 뒷받침 
Agent AI는 메모리 
수요를 증가시킨다 
HBM4 안정적 진입한 
것으로 판단

Topics  Loss‑making firms are on the rise in China's growth sectors

In China, the number of companies operating in the red is increasing. And the rise is particularly striking in growth 
sectors. One factor behind the increase is the intense competition among firms, which has led to a situation in which 
only the strongest thrive. Another is that many next‑generation growth companies, despite being viewed as having great 
potential, are losing money.

■ Loss‑making firms are on the rise in growth sectors 
In recent years, the number of companies in China's 
growth sectors that are operating in the red has 
increased markedly. The share of loss-making firms in 
all listed firms (below, "loss‑making‑firm ratio") rose 
sharply from 9.6% in 2019 to 26.9% in 2025. Looking 
at the industry distribution of loss‑making firms, from 
2019 to 2022, the ratio increased mainly in 
non‑manufacturing sectors such as real estate and 
accommodation and eating/drinking services, 
reflecting the property market downturn and strict 
stay‑at‑home measures implemented to curb the spread 
of COVID‑19. Later, from 2022 to 2025, the 
loss‑making-firm ratio surged in 
information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices; general, production, and 
commercial machinery; electrical machine ry; and 
chemicals. These four sectors alone accounted for 
around 60% of the overall increase. 
They are the principal growth sectors in Chinese 
manufacturing. This is evidenced by the fact that the 
growth rates of value added in all four sectors exceed 
the average for Chinese manufacturing. Many of the 
high‑tech manufacturing industries that the Chi nese 
government has prioritized for development are 
included in these sectors. For example, 5G base 
stations and semiconductors fall under 
information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices. And robots fall under general, 
production, and com mercial machinery. Meanwhile, 
solar cells and batteries fall under electrical machinery, 
and biopharmaceuticals and organic chemical 
materials fall under chemicals.  
Looking at recent developments in the 
manufacturing of information/communications 
equipment and electronic parts/devices, the global 
communications standard began shifting from 4G 
(LTE) to 5G services in around 2019, and ChatGPT was released in November 2022. These developments 
have provided tailwinds for the growth of the sector. Recently, as it has become clear that ordinary 
consumers and companies can make use of AI services on a daily basis, demand for AI‑related devices and 
semiconductors, as well as d ata centers, has soared. In this environment, many firms are focusing on 
strengthening development, manufacturing, and sales, and the sector's value added has continued to 
increase, rising from 780 billion yuan in 2022 to 910 billion yuan in 2025. Yet over  the same period, the 
number of loss‑making firms increased from 113 to 198, and the loss‑making‑firm ratio jumped from 17.2% 
to 30.1%. 
■ Competition among companies means only the strongest thrive 
One reason loss‑making firms are increasing despite operating in growth sectors is the intense 
competition among companies. The fight for market share has led to overheated investment competition

### Page 3

ASIA MONTHLY REPORT  October 2026, No.307

The Japan Research Institute, Limited  
Economics Department

2 
and price competition. In information/communications 
equipment and electronic parts/devices manufacturing, 
the rate of growth of non -current assets from 2022 to 
2025 (+35.4%) far exceeded the growth rate of value 
added (+16.9%). This indicates that supply capacity is 
expanding faster than demand. In addition, the 
consumer price index (CPI) increased by just 0.4% 
over the same period. Service prices rose 2.2%, 
reflecting wage increases, while goods prices fell 0.7% 
due to excess supply in manufacturing. 
Information/communications equipment prices 
decreased by 3.5%, larger than the drop for goods 
overall. 
As the selection of the fittest through competition 
among companies has progressed, profits have become 
concentrated at a small number of highly competitive 
firms. The share of profits earned by the top 1% of 
firms in information/communications equipment and electronic parts/devices manufacturing rose sharply 
from 24.0% in 2022 to 43.3% in 2025. Leading companies are simultaneously establishing positions as 
both "domestic champions" and "global champions."  
■ Next‑generation growth companies are viewed as having great potential even though they 
are losing money 
However, loss‑making firms cannot simply be lumped together and labeled as losers. Globally, investors 
have shown increasing interest recently in companies with high growth potential even if they are operating 
in the red, and China has a considerable number of such firms. 
In information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices manufacturing, one out of 
every two loss‑making firms is a company attracting 
strong investor interest. The Shanghai Composite 
Index rose 28.5% from 3,089 points at the beginning of 
2023 to 3,969 points at the end of 2025. 20 of the 198 
loss‑making firms in 2025 were not yet listed in 2023, 
and if these are excluded, there were 178 companies 
operating in the red as of 2025. Of these 178 firms, 96 
(53.9%) saw stock‑price increases over the period 
2023-2025 that exceeded the rise in the Shanghai 
Composite Index.  
One characteristic of these 96 companies with strong 
relative stock‑price performance is their active 
investment in R&D and intangible assets. Their 
average R&D intensity (R&D investment / sales) in 
2022 was 9.6%, and their ratio of intangible assets to 
value added stood at 60.5%. Intangible assets include 
computer software, patents, copyrights, and 
relationships with customers and suppliers. In contrast, 
the 82 loss‑making firms with weak relative 
stock‑price performance had an R&D intensity of only 
6.5% and an intangible‑asset‑to‑value‑added ratio of just 38.9%. 
As the above has shown, the increase in loss‑making firms in China's growth sectors is notable, and the 
factors behind it include not only the selection of the fittest as a result of competition among companies but 
also the emergence of next‑generation growth firms that are viewed as having great potential. 
  (Shinichi Seki)

Topics  Climate change heightens the risk of growth stagnation in Asia

Climate change could elevate the risk of economic growth flatlining in the ASEAN zone and India by causing labor 
productivity growth and capital accumulation to stall. To mitigate or avert such a scenario, establishing a business 
environment and institutions that promote "adaptation investment" by both the public and private sectors is an urgent 
task.

■ Heavy‑rain and flood damage is becoming increasingly serious in Asia 
Huge downpours and floods 
resulting in human casualties have 
been a frequent occurrence in Asia in 
the past several years. Still fresh in the 
memory is the debris flow that 
occurred in August this year near the 
border between Nepal and China, 
which as of t he time of writing has 
been recognized as a major disaster 
with thousands dead or missing. And 
in November of last year, Southeast 
Asia and South Asia saw 
record‑breaking torrential rains, 
resulting in numerous casualties, 
particularly in Indonesia and Sri  
Lanka. 
One factor that heightens the risks 
of such floods and heavy rains is 
climate change. As temperatures rise, 
the amount of water vapor in the 
atmosphere increases. Therefore, 
global warming could intensify 
torrential rainfall. Although such risk 
is shared a cross Asia, the way its 
effects are felt differs by region. 
Within Asia, the ASEAN zone and 
India are heavily affected by 
monsoons (seasonal winds), with 
many areas experiencing concentrated 
rainfall during the wet season. In such 
areas, warming can furthe r intensify 
monsoon‑driven heavy rains, 
increasing the risks of floods and 
landslides.  
■ Climate change will delay the escape from the "middle‑income trap" 
Climate change not only results in human casualties by way of natural disasters, but may also undermine 
the long‑term economic growth potential of ASEAN and India through various channels. The Asian 
Development Bank (ADB) [2024] conducted a quantitative an alysis of the extent to which climate change 
will weigh on Asian economies over the long term. Based on the IPCC's "very high GHG emissions" 
scenario (SSP5‑8.5), the analysis assumes a 4.7°C rise in the Earth's average temperature by 2100 relative 
to pre-industrial levels, and estimates the difference in real GDP between that and a hypothetical scenario 
without climate change. The results illustrate the long‑term impact on economic growth through declines 
in labor productivity, stagnation in capital accumulation, and downw ard pressure on consumption due to 
sea‑level rise, floods, heat waves, and other phenomena caused by climate change.  
An examination of the results reveals that in every country/territory, the pushdown effect on real GDP 
relative to the no‑climate‑change scenario expands over time, with 2035, 2050, and 2070 each seeing 
greater impact. The pushdown effect for ASEAN and Ind ia is −15.0% to −8.8% in 2050 and −30.2% to

4 
−18.1% in 2070, larger than the effects for China (2050: −4.8%, 2070: −10.3%) and Central Asian countries 
(2050: −1.7%, 2070: −3.8%). Within Asia, the ASEAN zone and India can be considered regions where the 
risk of economic stagnation due to climate change is relatively large.  
This may make it even more difficult for ASEAN countries and India to escape the "middle‑income trap." 
Although their income levels have risen, they still remain far from high‑income status. Indonesia, Malaysia, 
the Philippines, and Thailand became middle‑income countries in the 1990s or earlier, but have still not 
been able to join the groups of high‑income countries. Furthermore, Vietnam and India, which became 
middle‑income countries in the 2000s or later, have yet to enter a high‑growth trajectory like China once 
did. Climate change, by causing labor productivity growth to stall amid rising temperatures and companies 
to curtail investment due to more frequent disasters, may lead to income growth in the ASEAN zone and 
India being delayed further.  
■ Improving the environment to accelerate "adaptation investment" will be important 
To alleviate the pushdown effect of climate change on growth, expansion of "adaptation investment" by 
the public and private sectors is essential. Specifically, this could include improving working environments 
and promoting measures to lighten workloads a nd reduce headcounts in order to offset declines in labor 
productivity caused by rising temperatures, as well as developing infrastructure and strengthening facilities 
to prevent damage to production equipment from sea‑level rise and floods. However, ASEAN  and India 
cannot be said to have sufficiently 
developed the foundations for public 
and private promotion of such 
adaptation investment.  
As part of its Global Adaptation 
Initiative (ND‑GAIN), the University of 
Notre Dame (U.S.) evaluates countries' 
ability to use investment to achieve 
climate‑change adaptation in the form of 
a "readiness" score with three 
components: economic readiness, 
governance readiness, and social 
readiness. Respectively, these 
components assess 1) whether a 
business environment conducive to 
adaptation investment by private -sector 
companies has been put in place, 2) 
whether the effectiveness of institutions

5 
and policies to support private-sector adaptation investment has been secured, and 3) whether tangible and 
intangible social infrastructure that promotes investment has been established. 
Looking at readiness in 2024, ASEAN countries and India generally rank in the middle to lower tiers 
globally, with a large gap between them and developed countries. Notably, the Philippines, India, and 
Thailand fall outside the top 100, indicating weak momentum for adaptation investment. Breaking down 
the scores, problems are especially evident in the economic and governance categories. India and Thailand 
have particularly low eco nomic readiness, ranking 113th and 124th, respectively, and five countries 
(Malaysia not included) fall outside the top 100 for governance readiness. Given this situation, to accelerate 
private‑sector adaptation investment, India and Thailand in particular  need to move swiftly to improve the 
financial environment to make it easier to procure necessary funds domestically and internationally, relax 
regulations to encourage the launch of new businesses, and reform labor markets to enable the smooth 
securing of human resources. In addition, for Asia as a whole, it will also be important to boost confidence 
in the institutional factors that enhance application of investment for adaptation by increasing policy 
stability, ensuring the effectiveness of legal systems, and curbing corruption.

(Tomohiro Hosoi)

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -2.62 điểm tương ứng -0.15%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Công nghệ và Máy móc là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -13,987 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã HDB, PNJ và VJC; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-30 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều mua ròng 1,267 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua nhưng đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -2,363 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 3,726 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -2,702 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam trải qua một tuần đi ngang giằng co, giảm nhẹ và đóng cửa ở 1.735 điểm 
với thanh khoản ở mức thấp, và nhà đầu tư nước ngoài bán ròng cả 5 phiên trong tuần. 
Thông tin vĩ mô tích cực với tăng trưởng GDP quý 3 đạt mức gần 10% là điểm sáng trong 
tuần qua, tuy đã không đủ nâng đỡ thị trường chung. Thay vào đó, tâm lý thị trường thận 
trọng trước số liệu lạm phát ở mức cao, đợt tăng giá xăng dầu trong nước và mặt bằng lãi 
suất trong nước chưa có dấu hiệu hạ nhiệt. Diễn biến tăng của giá dầu thế giới, và lợi suất 
trái phiếu Chính phủ Mỹ cũng là các yếu tố gây áp lực lên thị trường chung. Tâm lý thận 
trọng còn thể hiện qua áp lực bán giải chấp ở một số cổ phiếu riêng lẻ như PNJ, SHB..., dù 
một vài nhóm trụ như dầu khí, tiêu dùng có thời điểm đóng vai trò nâng đỡ chỉ số.

Trần Đức Anh 
Giám đốc Kinh tế Vĩ mô & Chiến lược thị trường 
anhtd@kbsec.com.vn 
07/10/2026

Triển vọng TTCK 4Q2026 
Tích lũy trong vùng nhiễu động

Chúng tôi dự báo vùng điểm hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm 2026 
xuống 1,800 đi ểm, giảm mạnh từ mức 1,950 đi ểm đưa ra trong báo cáo gần nhất. 
Vùng điểm này tương ứng:

- Tăng trưởng EPS 2026 so với cùng kỳ của thị trường chung ở mức 33% 
(tăng mạnh so với mức 25% của báo cáo gần nhất), chủ yếu nhờ kỳ vọng 
vào các chính sách thúc đẩy kinh tế của Chính phủ, và lợi nhuận tăng 
trưởng đột biến ở VIC (xem thêm phần Triển vọng hoạt động kinh doanh).

- Mức P/E hợp lý của chỉ số VNIndex thời điểm cuối năm ở mức 13.2 (giảm 
nhẹ so với mức 13.5 thời điểm hiện tại - phản ánh quan điểm các yếu tố 
rủi ro sẽ tiếp tục duy trì). Đây là mức định giá thấp so với bình quân 5 năm 
gần nhất ở 15.1. Nếu loại bỏ yếu tố đột biến ở nhóm Vingroup, chỉ số P/E 
điều chỉnh hiện đã rơi xuống 10.9, thấp hơn các đáy gần nhất năm 2020, 
2022 và 2025. Trong bối cảnh kinh tế Việt Nam duy trì tốc độ tăng trưởng 
cao, lợi nhuận các doanh nghiệp niêm yết liên tục bứt phá, vùng định giá 
này mở ra cơ hội đầu tư trung/dài hạn. Chúng tôi cho rằng ngay khi các rủi 
ro vĩ mô quốc tế (xoay quanh vấn đề Iran), và trong nước (xoay quanh vấn 
đề lãi suất) hạ nhiệt, thị trường chung sẽ có cơ hội tái định giá lên mức cao 
hơn đáng kể so với thời điểm hiện tại. Dù vậy, khác với báo cáo gần nhất 
vào tháng 7, ở báo cáo này, chúng tôi đẩy lui thời điểm các yếu tố rủi ro vĩ 
mô hạ nhiệt sang năm 2027, đồng nghĩa với quan điểm thận trọng hơn về 
thị trường cho quý 4 2026.

Cụ thể, đối với vấn đề lãi suất, áp lực lạm phát lớn dần, đi kèm nhu cầu vốn cao phục 
vụ hoạt động đầu tư cơ sở hạ tầng sẽ khiến mặt bằng lãi suất duy trì ở mức cao cho 
phần còn lại của năm 2026 (dù thanh khoản hệ thống ghi nhận sự cải thiện đáng kể 
trong 1 quý gần đây). Đối với vấn đề xung đột Trung Đông, các cuộc đàm phán vẫn 
đang bế tắc và ít có kh ả năng dòng ch ảy dầu mỏ tại khu vực này được nối lại ngay 
trong năm nay.

Chủ đề đầu tư chúng tôi l ựa chọn cho 4Q2026 là đ ầu tư công, chính sách ti ền tệ hỗ 
trợ, và xung đột Iran.

Xem đầy đủ tại đây.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành mẫu nến Doji trên khung đồ thị tuần sau hai tuần giảm điểm liên tiếp, 
cho thấy áp lực bán đã phần nào được kiểm soát. Trên khung ngày, nỗ lực hồi phục đầu 
tuần gặp lực cản tại đường SMA50 khiến VNINDEX quay đầu giảm điểm, tiếp tục chớm phá 
đáy ngắn hạn; tuy nhiên vùng hỗ trợ gần đã tạo hiệu ứng thu hút lực cầu bắt đáy, giúp chỉ 
số thu hẹp đà giảm trong phiên cuối tuần. Sau nhịp điều chỉnh khá tiêu cực từ vùng đỉnh 
đầu tháng 9, VNINDEX đang bước vào giai đoạn tìm kiếm điểm cân bằng trở lại và diễn biến 
giằng co điều chỉnh có thể còn kéo dài thêm trong các phiên kế tiếp. Tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn tính từ vùng đáy cuối tháng 7 vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại tại điểm đỡ gần quanh 1710 (đây cũng là chốt chặn cần 
giữ để bảo vệ được xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn).

Document 325: CoreWeave大涨给AI云“喘息之机”，接下来就看Anthropic IPO和英伟达了

Source PDF: all report pdf/CoreWeave大涨给AI云“喘息之机”，接下来就看Anthropic IPO和英伟达了.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

CoreWeave 三季报营收与指引双双超预期，单⽇股价暴涨 25% ，带动新云板块飙升逾
12% 。 Anthropic 传出 1.5 万亿美元 IPO 估值、 OpenAI 单季营收⾸破百亿美元，三重利好共
振，提振了此前深陷 AI 回报疑虑的算⼒产业链。下周英伟达财报与 Anthropic 上市，将是这
轮反弹能否⾛远的真正试⾦⽯。
摘要：
CoreWeave 周⼆发布三季度财报，营收和亏损均好于预期，全年指引亦超出市场预期上限。消
息提振了此前因 AI 投资回报担忧⽽持续承压的 “ 新云 ” （ neocloud ）板块。
在 CoreWeave 股价单⽇⼤涨约 25% 的带动下，新云（ NeoCloud ）板块盘中涨幅⼀度超过 12% ，
纳指也收复了中期选举后的失地。市场的乐观情绪还受到两则消息强化： Anthropic 据报正讨论
⾼达 1.5 万亿美元以上的 IPO 估值，打消了此前公司估值将⼤幅下调的担忧；此外， OpenAI 单季
营收强劲、⾸次达到 100 亿美元。

⽽对于即将在下周登陆资本市场的 Anthropic ⽽⾔，这份财报来得恰逢其时 ——CoreWeave 是
Anthropic 的重要算⼒供应商，两家公司的命运在⼀定程度上相互绑定。与此同时，英伟达下周
的财报将是另⼀块试⾦⽯，投资者届时将寻找更多理由判断 AI 基础设施热潮能否持续。
不过，这轮反弹的基础仍不牢固：⼀⽅⾯，资⾦正从超⼤规模云⼚商转向 “ 卖铲⼈ ” 和算⼒租赁
商；另⼀⽅⾯， CoreWeave 未来的融资缺⼝、债务成本及算⼒供给扩张，仍是决定这轮 AI 基建
交易能否⾛远的核⼼变量。
CoreWeave 带动新云板块集体反弹
CoreWeave 三季度营收 36.6 亿美元，超出分析师预期；每股亏损 1.27 美元，亏损幅度⼩于市场
此前担忧的⽔平。更受关注的是全年指引：公司预计 2026 年全年营收在 130 亿⾄ 133 亿美元之
间，⾼于市场⼀致预期上限约 3.4% 。
CoreWeave CEO Michael Intrator 在与分析师的电话会议上透露了⼀个值得关注的细节：今年

新增客⼾中，超过⼀半来⾃ AI 领域以外。这⼀数据在⼀定程度上回应了市场对 AI 算⼒需求过度集
中、⼀旦 AI 投资热潮退却便将⾯临需求断崖的担忧。 Intrator 的表述暗⽰，对⾼性能算⼒的需求
正在向更⼴泛的⾏业渗透，⽽不仅仅依赖 AI 实验室的采购。
在此之前， CoreWeave 股价年内已累计下跌 39% ，是 AI 算⼒板块中跌幅最深的个股之⼀。德意
志银⾏分析师 Brad Zelnick 在财报发布后表⽰，感谢公司提供了 “ 令⼈宽慰的细节 ” ，这句话颇能
代表市场整体情绪。
财报公布后， CoreWeave 股价单⽇⼤涨约 25% ，成为带动新云板块反弹的核⼼标的。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

市场数据显⽰，新云概念股当⽇整体上涨逾 12%，⽽⾼盛⼴义 AI 股票篮⼦上涨约 5% ，显著跑赢
⾮ AI 股票。此前受 AI 资本开⽀回报疑虑拖累的算⼒产业链，迎来了⼀轮快速估值修复。
纳指也收复了中期选举后的失地。

### Page 3

但从市场交易结构看，资⾦的选择也反映出投资者尚未完全消除疑虑。算⼒租赁商、半导体企
业等 AI 资本开⽀的 “ 收款⽅ ” 表现更强；微软、⾕歌、亚⻢逊等承担巨额数据中⼼投资的超⼤规模
云⼚商则相对落后。换⾔之，市场眼下更愿意押注 AI 建设周期的直接受益者，⽽⾮继续为巨额资
本开⽀买单的 “ 付款⽅ ” 。
CoreWeave 与 Anthropic ：命运相连
⽽ CoreWeave 的财报对 Anthropic ⽽⾔具有直接意义。
Anthropic 是 CoreWeave 的客⼾，使⽤ CoreWeave 及其他多家新云服务商的算⼒来⽀撑其
Claude 系列产品，同时也与⾕歌等超⼤规模云⼚商签有合同。两家公司从未公开披露合同条
款，但 Intrator 今年 4 ⽉曾将与 Anthropic 的合作描述为 “ 数⼗亿美元级别的合同 ” 。
据彭博报道， Anthropic 计划于下周完成上市。作为⾸家寻求公开上市的美国前沿 AI 实验室，
Anthropic 没有搜索引擎、社交媒体或云业务等传统业务作为 “ 护城河 ” ，其估值将完全取决于市场

对 AI 前景的判断。 CoreWeave 的强劲表现，理论上有助于 Anthropic 在路演中获得更⾼的估值认
可。
最新市场消息进⼀步抬⾼了这场 IPO 的关注度：据报道， Anthropic 的承销商正评估 1.5 万亿美元
或更⾼的 IPO 估值可能性。若该估值预期得以维持， Anthropic 将不只是检验前沿⼤模型公司商
业化能⼒的样本，也将成为市场为 AI ⻓期增⻓故事愿意⽀付何种溢价的直接标尺。此前有消息称
Anthropic 估值可能⼤打折扣，或在 1.5 万亿美元以下。
与此同时， OpenAI 单季营收⾸次达到 100 亿美元的消息，也为 AI 应⽤层需求提供了新的⽀撑。
对投资者⽽⾔，这意味着市场正在尝试寻找 “ 模型收⼊增⻓ — 算⼒采购扩张 — 芯⽚及数据中⼼投
资回报 ” 之间能够相互验证的闭环；但这⼀闭环是否⾜以⽀撑⾼估值，仍有待更多公司财报和订
单数据验证。
对 Anthropic ⽽⾔， CoreWeave 财报释放的积极信号在于：其上游算⼒供应商仍能获得强劲订

单、提⾼收⼊指引并维持融资能⼒。但这也凸显了双⽅的共同⻛险 —— ⼤模型公司对外部算⼒
的需求能否持续⾼增⻓，以及算⼒供应商能否在⾼资本开⽀和⾼负债模式下保持稳定交付，将
共同影响这条产业链的估值基础。值得注意的是， Anthropic 在算⼒供应上还依赖其他⼏家前景
尚不明朗的新云服务商，其中包括 Nscale—— 据报道该公司正在考虑推迟⾃⾝的 IPO 计划。
AI 下⼀个考验：英伟达财报

### Page 4

下周，英伟达将发布财报。如果投资者能够暂时搁置对 CEO ⻩仁勋及其他相关问题的担忧，英
伟达的业绩或将为市场提供更多乐观理由。
英伟达财报的重要性，已不⽌于验证 GPU 需求是否旺盛。市场更希望从中确认三件事：云⼚商
和新云服务商的采购强度是否持续、 Blackwell 等新产品的供给与交付能否匹配需求，以及客⼾
资本开⽀的快速增⻓能否转化为更可持续的收⼊和利润。
本轮反弹中，半导体板块表现强劲，费城半导体指数录得数⽉来最⼤单⽇涨幅，并收复此前⼀
周回调的⼤部分失地；英伟达股价也出现反弹。不过，交易员观察到，部分活跃资⾦和散⼾在
英伟达财报前仍有卖出⾏为。这意味着市场对芯⽚⻰头的预期依旧很⾼，财报若⽆法提供⾜够
强的指引， AI 交易的波动可能再度放⼤。
彭博专栏作家 Dave Lee 分析，早在 2025 年 3 ⽉ CoreWeave 上市之际，他便曾警告这家公司将成
为 “AI ⾰命的第⼀只真正的⻛向标股票 ” ，事实证明确实如此 ——CoreWeave 的股价已成为市场波
动的主要 “ 出⽓筒 ” 。

反弹背后：融资缺⼝与供给扩张仍是硬约束
不过，这轮反弹的基础仍不牢固。 CoreWeave 未来的融资缺⼝、债务成本及算⼒供给扩张，仍
是决定这轮 AI 基建交易能否⾛远的核⼼变量。
CoreWeave 的业绩缓解了 “AI 建设会否因融资受阻⽽戛然⽽⽌ ” 的短期担忧，但并未彻底回答⻓
期问题。
多头逻辑是，CoreWeave 可以围绕客⼾合同设⽴融资主体，以⾼⽐例担保债务覆盖建设成本；
在合同期内偿还债务后，公司仍保留 GPU 资产，并可将已还清融资的设备再次出租。若算⼒⻓
期紧缺，这种模式有望令存量设备持续产⽣现⾦流。
空头则聚焦三个⻛险。⾸先是定价：管理层所提及的每兆⽡收⼊⽔平，与财务报表所反映的实
际变现⽔平之间仍存在差距。其次是融资：市场观点认为，公司明年或⾯临约 330 亿美元的资⾦
缺⼝，仍需依赖新增债务或客⼾预付款填补。最后是供给：到 2028 年前，市场预计将有约 55 吉
⽡新增算⼒容量投放，若供给释放快于需求增⻓，⽼⼀代 GPU 的租⾦和残值都可能承压。
因此， CoreWeave 本季证明的是，在算⼒依然紧张时，公司能够拿到订单并获得融资；但它尚

⽆法证明，供给紧张会持续到⾜以覆盖巨额债务、并⽀撑旧 GPU 残值的阶段。接下来，市场将
密切关注其投资级担保贷款的最终定价、客⼾预付款规模，以及公司资本开⽀和⾃由现⾦流拐
点能否如预期出现。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
宏观环境并未放⾏，监管不确定性仍是隐患
在美国中期选举后，⺠主党控制国会，德克萨斯州候任州⻓ Gina Hinojosa 在竞选期间曾主张对
数据中⼼建设实施暂停令。 Intrator 上周已致信 Hinojosa ，希望就已获批项⽬的 “ 负责任完⼯ ” 以及
未来项⽬的 “ 持久标准 ” 寻求共识。
在财报电话会议上， Intrator 试图稳定投资者情绪。他表⽰： “ 我们预计不会受到近期重⼤监管变
化的影响。我们认为，这些变化是关于如何建设的表态，⽽⾮是否建设。 ”
除监管外，利率仍可能是 AI 基础设施估值的另⼀重约束。在本轮股市反弹期间，市场对未来加
息的定价有所上升， 10 年期美债期货隐含收益率重新逼近 5% 。对于依赖债务融资建设数据中⼼
的新云公司⽽⾔，融资成本上升会直接影响项⽬回报率和股权估值。

更重要的是，股票、信⽤、利率和原油市场的波动率尚未完全回落。此前建⽴的避险仓位虽在
本轮反弹中部分平仓， VIX ⼀度回落⾄ 17 附近，但跨资产波动仍处于相对偏⾼⽔平。这意味着，
CoreWeave 带来的 “ 喘息之机 ” 能否演变成趋势性修复，仍取决于后续财报、融资市场和宏观利
率环境能否同时给出积极答案。
总体⽽⾔， CoreWeave 财报暂时修复了市场对 AI 算⼒需求和融资能⼒的信⼼，也为 Anthropic
IPO 及英伟达财报营造了更有利的情绪窗⼝。但从 “ 短期空头回补 ” ⾛向 “ ⻓期基本⾯重估 ” ，仍需
回答融资、供给、 GPU 残值和利率四道难题。下周的 Anthropic 与英伟达，将是这轮 AI 反弹能否
延续的两场关键考试。

Document 356: FX volatility in 2027_ In need of shock therapy

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX volatility in 2027_ In need of shock therapy.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

Why is FX volatility so low? Even a 6% fall in the Nasdaq barely
budges USD/JPY these days. Have FX markets found a general
equilibrium, or is there some kind of separation between
geopolitics and macro? We think the answer lies more in the
structure of FX trading and do not rule out jump risk if the
shock is big enough

Chris Turner, Francesco Pesole and Dmitry Dolgin
Whatever happened to FX volatility?
Despite wars in the Middle East and Continental Europe, an energy-
driven inﬂation spike and growing fears over an AI bubble, FX
volatility is close to its lowest levels in over a decade. If moves in
spot exchange rates are the ultimate driver of traded volatility, then
the recent stasis in spot USD/JPY in the face of a 6% drop in the
Nasdaq tells the story. In short, if exchange rates aren’t moving,
why should the market pay up for volatility?
It is not as though FX volatility is low in all markets. AI concerns
have seen equity volatility spike, while surging energy prices,

### Page 2

central bank tightening, and fears about debt sustainability have
all seen interest rate volatility remain elevated.
FX volatility has remained muted
Source: ING, Reﬁnitiv
The lack of spot FX movement or spot follow-through cannot
simply be blamed on some kind of separation between geopolitics
and macro. This year’s war in Iran has driven a massive hawkish
repricing at the short end of yield curves and turned easing cycles
on their heads. And in this outlook, we argue that if there is one

theme that FX markets should take seriously, it’s global monetary
policy cycles.
Any pickup in FX volatility more likely from
equities than bonds
One of our key calls for 2027 is that we don’t expect a signiﬁcant
pickup in FX volatility. Markets have built up considerable tolerance
for US policy and geopolitical headlines, and may be even more
willing to fade US political risk if a Democrat-led Congress and
weaker Republican support constrain US President Donald Trump’s

room for aggressive policy action.
If volatility does break higher, we think equities are a more likely
trigger than rates. Historical evidence supports that view, with
equity volatility consistently showing greater pass-through into FX
than rates volatility (chart below).

### Page 3

Sticky global inﬂation and prolonged monetary tightening are
more plausible risks than a major equity selloﬀ. Yet we don’t think
even a high-inﬂation scenario would be enough to recreate 2022-
2023 levels of FX volatility. Policy rates entered the Iran war from
much higher levels than at the end of the pandemic, while ﬁscal
support is smaller and labour markets are looser. The ingredients
for another 2022-23-style rates shock simply look less potent.

Greater FX shock potential from equities
Source: ING, Reﬁnitiv
Structural factors: Growth in ﬁnancial
assets playing a role?
When thinking about factors that could be suppressing volatility in
spot exchange rates, we suspect the growth in the asset
management industry could be playing a role. Both assets under
management and cross-border positions have continued to surge
over recent years. Using World Bank and Bank for International
Settlements (BIS) data, we now estimate that global holdings of

equities and bonds are worth around 300% of global GDP. As the BIS
reported back in 2025, FX trading volumes were about 70 times the
volume of global trade in 2025, roughly double the ratio of 1992.
These ﬁnancial FX ﬂows, including portfolio hedging and
rebalancing, can be an important source of FX liquidity when they
are gradual and diversiﬁed. But correlated hedging or rebalancing

### Page 4

can amplify FX moves. The latter was the case during the Trump
tariﬀ shock in March/April 2025.
Stock of global equity and ﬁxed income
holdings
Source: World Bank, BIS, ING
Structural factors: FX engineering playing a
role?
One of the big trends in FX trading over recent years has been the
‘internalisation’ of FX ﬂows – the concept being that a bank can
deliver steadier, better pricing for customers by oﬀsetting client
ﬂow with other internal client ﬂow/hedging activity without having

to go to external liquidity venues.
We suspect there is quite a lot of overstatement of the extent of
‘internalisation’ out there, where banks may be including dealing
on ‘lit’ venues (transparent order books) as part of their
internalisation activity. Undoubtedly, the advent of non-bank
liquidity providers to lit venues has been a major contributor to the
depth of market pricing, as has the growth of algorithmic pricing
engines. The design of these algo pricing engines, which pull back

from putting large volumes through in thin conditions, has
probably contributed to calmer conditions, too. Indeed, it seems
that when volatility conditions pick up, the buy-side prefers to use
algos to execute rather than paying the wider spreads associated
with principal liquidity transfers.

But before we conclude that FX volatility is dead, evidence shows
that major events – Liberation Day in April 2025, or the start of the
Iran war in February 2026 – can still have a big say in FX markets.
Below, we show some data on the depth of the EUR/USD market
starting in 2025. The chart shows the impact on EUR/USD, in USD

pips, one minute after a trade has gone through. We show the
impact of four size buckets, equally weighted: $0-1m, $1-5m, $5-
10m and $10-30m.
Big events do still impact market depth, which could be due to
some non-banks pulling liquidity from lit venues.
So, while the above factors could be contributing to lower volatility,
our conclusion is that volatility is not dead and jump risk or fat tails
will still be a fact of life in FX markets.

Standard Chartered Bank

Charts of the week: Watching earnings and a potential ‘Blue wave’

US Big Tech earnings, mid-term elections and major central bank policy meetings are the near-term market drivers 
Consensus S&P500 index Q3 earnings estimates by sector   Chance of Democrats winning control of US House and Senate*

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered; *Polymarket probability

Editorial 
Policy, politics and earnings  
Strategy summary:  We retain our near -term defensive and 
diversified stance across assets, sectors and regions amid 
near-term uncertainty around monetary policy, politics and 
earnings. The next two weeks will be busy for major central 
banks, US political watchers and technology leaders powering 
the AI supercycle. We expect the Fed, ECB and BoJ to hold 
rates next week, but markets will focus on whether central 
banks are turning more hawkish due to the latest surge in fuel 
prices. The US mid -term elections are expected to give 
Democrats control of the House and Senate, with implications 
for taxes and the AI-led investment boom.  
Meanwhile, the consensus is expecting the US tech sector to 
report over 60% y/y earnings growth for Q3, the biggest jump 
in over two decades outside the post-COVID rebound.  Each of 
these three drivers have the potential to surprise markets.  
Big Three to hold monetary policy: We expect the Fed, ECB 
and BoJ to hold policy rates next week, after September’s 
hikes, as they assess whether higher bond yields have 
tightened financial conditions enough to offset fuel-price-driven 
inflation. We expect the Big Three to hike next in December as 
fuel prices are likely to stay elevated into early next year due to 
refining capacity constraints. Nevertheless, the Fed and ECB 
are likely to deliver less than around 60bps of rate hikes priced 
in by money markets over the next 12 months on the back of 
an eventual easing of oil and fuel prices leading to disinflation 
by H2 2027. This outlook means 3 -7-year Developed Market 
investment grade bonds currently offer attractive risk-reward.  
Opportunity in Australian bonds. Other major central banks, 
such as the Bank of England, Bank of Canada and Reserve 
Bank of New Zealand are likely to hike rates by 25bps in the 
next two weeks given their focus on taming near-term inflation. 
However, we believe the Reserve Bank of Australia (RBA) is 
close to the end of its hiking cycle, even if it hikes one last time 
in the coming months (money markets give a 60% chance of a 
hike on 3 November). Australia’s domestic labour and housing 
markets and business confidence are softening after the RBA 
hiked its policy rate four times this year to a 15 -year high. 
Meanwhile, Australia’s cash rate is 60bps higher than the US 
policy rate, supporting the AUD. We continue to see attractive 
income and FX gain opportunities from AUD corporate bonds.  
A ‘Blue Wave’ in the US?  Prediction markets strongly favour 
Democrats taking the House of Representatives and possibly 
the Senate in the 3 November US mid-term elections. While an 
opposition sweep in the mid-terms has historically delivered the 
best 12-month returns for equities  among all sc enarios, partly 
due to the resulting policy gridlock, a decisive Democratic “blue 
wave” could increase longer -term risks of higher taxes and 
uncertainty over policy towards AI investment and data centres. 
With the Senate race still close and prediction markets fa llible, 
we remain cautious on risk assets in the near term.  
Closing EU bank contingent convertible bond s (CoCos1) 
and US 10 -year go vernment bond ideas:  We lock in 2% 
gains in EU CoCos and 1.7% gain in US 10 -year government 
bond ideas amid French and US political risks (page 5-6). We 
continue to hedge any inflation risks through inflation-protected 
bonds and geopolitical risk through an Overweight in gold.  
Watching Big Tech earnings:   Upcoming Big Tech earnings 
raises uncertainty as market leadership remains concentrated 
and share -price reactions more mixed. With valuations less 
likely to drive returns, earnings delivery and sustainability of AI 
investment, funding and monet isation and broadening out of 
earnings beyond tech will be critical, raising the risk of volatility 
and sector divergence.  We remain invested but defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification across sectors and regions.  
Staying defensive and diversified. We recently upgraded the 
defensive US Healthcare sector to Overweight , while retaining 
our Overweights to US tech and materials as well as financials 
in the US, Euro area and Japan. We also downgraded the more 
volatile Communications Services sector to Neutral  and took 
profits on the semiconductor segment earlier this month before 
the latest correction. Our defensive and diversified stance 
should preserve participation in further equity upside, while 
reducing exposure to earnings disappointments and supporting 
resilience through the reporting season.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions 
  What are the key takeaways from the technology sector 
earnings reported so far this week? 
Our view: Recent  technology sector earnings results point to 
strong compute demand and continued capex expansion, 
supporting our positive long -term view on the AI investment 
theme. However, rising funding and execution risks warrant 
near-term caution. We prefer to avoid increasing exposure to 
higher-risk areas within the technology sector for now. 
Rationale: Earnings results reported this week suggest major tech 
companies remain committed to expanding their AI infrastructure. 
While this bodes well for AI infrastructure providers, ambitious 
expansion plans imply that an increasing share of AI capex is likely 
to depend on debt financing. We expect debt-financed AI capex to 
rise from c.25% of AI capex in 2026 to c.40% in 2027.  
Greater reliance on borrowing increases sensitivity to interest rates, 
while deployment delays could postpone revenue recognition amid 
ongoing financing costs. However, evidence of improving AI 
monetisation and adoption should strengthen operating cash 
flows and support internal capex funding. Against this backdrop, 
we will assess Big Tech earnings next week for greater clarity on 
capex guidance and funding and AI monetisation trends.  
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  Are you still positive on Euro  area bank equities amid 
heightened political and fiscal uncertainty in the region? 
Our view: We remain positive on Euro  area bank equities, 
supported by resilient earnings and shareholder-friendly capital 
return policies. While Fr ance’s fiscal uncertainty and a softer 
growth outlook warrant caution, the recent pullback in Euro  
area bank equities  offers selective opportunities. We favour 
well-capitalised banks with strong deposit franchises and 
diversified earnings. 
Rationale: We expect Euro  area banks’ Q3 2026 net interest 
income to grow 1.5 -3.0% q/q , led by Spanish banks, driven by 
higher rates and continued asset repricing. Resilient fee income from 
wealth management and investment banking activity, as well as 
dividends and buybacks, should provide further support. Price-to-
book (P/B) valuations have eased , despite expectations for 
healthy return on equity (ROE), thus improving risk-reward. 
Fiscal uncertainty in France has widened sovereign spreads, 
pressuring banks’ bond holdings and funding costs . Although 
French banks represent 13% of the Euro  area Bank Index, volatility 
could spread to regional financial markets. However, stress in the 
broader banking system appears limited , supported by strong 
capital buffers and ECB tools to contain disorderly market moves and 
sovereign market contagion. We will continue to monitor credit 
trends, sovereign spreads and banks’ 2027 earnings guidance. 
—  Robin Xie, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What has driven the recent weakness in  China equities, 
and what is your outlook from here? 
Our view: We maintain China equities as  a Core holding , as 
valuations remain inexpensive. Rapid technological innovation 
and investment in  AI localisation provide tailwinds, but the 
potential for a re-rating appears delayed. Investors should seek 
to diversify their portfolios and reduce concentration risk. 
Rationale: China equities have weak ened, driven by mounting 
scepticism over AI capex returns, structural margin 
compression and concerns about the country’s significant 
financing requirements. Another notable feature in China has been 
significant share issuance, which has diluted earnings per share and 
weighed on the MSCI China Index’s performance. These pressures 
have been compounded by geopolitical headwinds, restrictions on 
advanced chips, an enduring property sect or contraction and weak 
domestic demand. September’s stimulus measures also 
disappointed markets in terms of their scale and pace of delivery. 
Notwithstanding these headwinds, valuations remain inexpensive, 
supported by rapid domestic innovation and structural tailwinds 
in AI localisation , leading us to retain China equities as a Core 
holding. Elevated volatility reinforces the need to be defensively 
positioned, favouring diversification rather than relying on technology 
sector concentration. We also remain Overweight China’s 
healthcare, financial and materials sectors. 
—  Cindy Lam, CFA, Senior Investment Strategist

Are you still bullish on US 10-year government bonds?   
Our view: We are closing our opportunistic bullish idea on US 
10-year government bonds at a gain of 1.7%. The risk -reward 
has become less compelling after the yield broke below 5%, the 
lower bound of our three-month forecast range of 5.00-5.25%. 
Rationale: The US 10 -year yield fell  over 17bps to 4.97% on 22 
October – its first move below 5% since 23 September and biggest 
one-day drop since March 2023. A sell-off in AI-related equities and 
renewed Middle East tensions drove investors towards safe havens. 
Earlier this month, the yield hit its highest level in over two decades. 
The forces behind the rally are fragile, though.  Oil prices remain 
elevated amid escalating Middle East tensions, and September US 
consumer inflation rose to 3.7% y/y . Fed officials  have signalled 
there is no urgency to raise rates at the 27-28 October meeting, 
but persistent price pressures keep further tightening on the table. 
We prefer to lock in gains and close our opportunistic bullish idea on 
10-year government bonds at a gain of 1.7%. 
—  Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO, Fixed Income & FX

Significant share issuance has kept the market 
capitalisation fairly stable, while the MSCI China 
Index has declined c.20% over the past one year

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is your outlook for French bank bonds , especially 
ahead of France’s upcoming sovereign credit rating reviews?   
Our view: The recent sell-off in French bank bonds is a repricing 
of tail risks, not a credit story. We expect further price volatility 
until the April 2027 French presidential election, particularly at 
the junior end of the capital structure, so we are closing our 
opportunistic EU bank contingent convertibles (CoCos1) idea at 
a gain of 2.0%. 
Rationale: We believe the recent sell -off in French bank bonds 
reflects domestic political and fiscal risks rather than weaker 
credit fundamentals . The sustained widening of the OAT -Bund 
spread has raised concerns about the profit and loss (P&L) impact 
on banks' sovereign debt holdings. Banks provide limited 
disclosure on these exposures. However, banks’ investment 
portfolios are generally diversified, and holdings booked at amortised 
cost should soften the P&L impact. Near -term catalysts include the 
upcoming sovereign credit rating rev iews. A sovereign downgrade 
could trigger bank rating downgrades, with domestically focused 
lenders potentially more exposed than regionally diversified peers. A 
sovereign credit event is not our base case, but we expect headline 
risks to persist until the  French presidential election . Against this 
backdrop, we are closing our opportunistic EU bank CoCos idea. 
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
  Will the November US mid-term elections and European 
bond volatility support a sustained Swiss franc (CHF) rebound?  
Our view: We expect a near -term CHF rebound, supported 
mainly by safe -haven demand amid European bond volatility. 
We believe the US mid-term elections will be a secondary driver. 
Rationale: European sovereign bond volatility provides the stronger 
catalyst for CHF strength. The Fr ance-Germany government bond 
yield spread remains elevated near 160bps, reflecting heightened 
French fiscal concerns and reinforcing the CHF’s safe-haven appeal. 
The US mid-terms could provide additional support for the CHF. 
A Democratic victory in either chamber is expected to  constrain 
further fiscal expansion, potentially weighing on the USD. However, 
this would not necessarily lead to faster fiscal consolidation. 
The SNB’s 0% policy rate leaves  the CHF at a sizeable interest -
rate disadvantage vs. the USD, constraining sustained CHF gains. 
With EUR/CHF below 0.90 , excessive CHF strength could also 
increase the risk of SNB intervention . A decisive USD/CHF break 
below 0.8130 would strengthen the technical case for a more 
sustained CHF rebound.  
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist

(IG) bond option-adjusted spread (OAS) suggest a 
repricing of political and fiscal risk rather than 
credit risk

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  Would worsening budget deficits and political uncertainty 
across parts of Europe affect your view on EUR/USD and Euro 
area government bond yields? 
Our view: Fiscal and political concerns constrain near -term 
EUR/USD gains, with France being the primary vulnerability. We 
expect EUR/USD to recover towards the 1.17-1.18 range over 
the next  12 months, supported by continued Euro area 
economic growth and an expected fading of USD support. We 
expect wider differentials in Euro area government bond yields, 
with political risk premium elevated in Fr ench government 
bonds (OATs) and German government bonds (Bunds) 
supported by safe-haven demand. 
Rationale: France's high  public debt, large budget deficit and 
political obstacles to fiscal adjustment s are  weighing on market 
confidence, with the country’s  10-year government bond yield 
rising above 5%. The risk of stress spreading to Italy and Spain 
adds to regional concerns, although current evidence does not 
establish a broad European fiscal crisis. German government bonds 
should benefit from safe-haven demand, while fiscal concerns keep 
risk premi a on vulnerable countries' bonds elevated. EUR/USD 
remains more sensitive to US-Germany interest-rate differentials 
and broader market confidence than to French bond yields alone. 
France’s fiscal risks, e levated energy costs and high US interest 
rates weigh on the EUR in the near term. Over the next 12 months, 
Germany’s multi -year public investment programme, resilient 
exports and sound banking-sector fundamentals are expected to 
support Euro area growth. We expect the Fed's rate-hiking cycle to 
peak by mid -2027, reducing USD support and driving  the EUR  
towards 1.17-1.18 . 
The principal tail risk  is fiscal stress widening government bond 
yield spreads and spilling over into bank funding  conditions and 
lending activity, weakening growth and delaying the EUR's recovery. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

### Page 1

内塔尼亚胡 10 ⽇在以⾊列议会的讲话凸显了美以双⽅在伊朗问题上仍存重⼤分歧。特朗普
政府希望尽⼀切努⼒与德⿊兰达成协议，⽽内塔尼亚胡则认为，若不推翻伊朗政权，核威
胁就⽆法永久消除。针对是否以及何时恢复对伊朗的⾏动，已成为美以之间紧张关系的核
⼼议题。
摘要：
据以⾊列 Ynet 新闻⽹等媒体 11 ⽇援引消息⼈⼠的话爆料，以⾊列总理内塔尼亚胡和美国总统特
朗普上周末就伊朗问题通了电话，这番通话异常艰难且充满⽕药味，⼀名消息⼈⼠甚⾄称之为
“ ⼀场地狱般的争吵 ” 。不过，以总理办公室否认了以美领导⼈发⽣激烈争吵的报道。
美国总统特朗普（左）和以⾊列总理内塔尼亚胡  资料图  图源：外媒
据 Ynet 新闻⽹报道，此次通话主要围绕伊朗问题展开，该通话的背景是，以美两国对是否重启
伊朗战事这⼀问题存在分歧。针对内塔尼亚胡和特朗普发⽣激烈争吵的说法，以总理办公室回
应称 “ 假新闻，纯属⽆稽之谈 ” ，并坚称通话内容并⽆对抗性。此外，⼀名以⾊列⾼级官员证实通
话确实发⽣过，但同样坚称 “ 这绝⾮⼀次艰难的谈话 ” 。

尽管如此，报道继续称，内塔尼亚胡 10 ⽇在以⾊列议会的讲话凸显了美以双⽅在伊朗问题上仍
存重⼤分歧。特朗普政府希望尽⼀切努⼒与德⿊兰达成协议，⽽内塔尼亚胡则认为，若不推翻
伊朗政权，核威胁就⽆法永久消除。针对是否以及何时恢复对伊朗的⾏动，已成为美以之间紧
张关系的核⼼议题。
美共和党中期选举惨败后，特朗普就伊朗战事的表态出现明显变化。 11 ⽉ 5 ⽇，他称美⽅将决定
是否要对伊朗进⼀步采取打击⾏动，以确保彻底终结伊朗核计划； 8 ⽇他话锋⼀转，称只要美伊
谈判取得进展，美国 “ 极不可能 ” 恢复对伊朗的⼤规模军事⾏动，与伊朗已接近达成协议， “ 没有
任何理由 ” 升级伊朗战事。伊朗⽅⾯则⽴场依旧强硬。伊朗最⾼国家安全委员会秘书穆赫⾟ · 雷扎
伊 10 ⽇在社交媒体发⽂称，针对伊朗的战争已经重创了美国政府， “ 是时候接受伊朗反复提出的
七项条件并选择投降了 ” 。伊朗外交部 9 ⽇再次强调，美国取消制裁、停⽌战争是启动谈判的前
提。
有分析称，美国强硬⽴场有所软化后，短期内美伊⼤规模战事重启的可能性有所下降，但双⽅

能否通过谈判打破当前 “ 不战不和 ” 的僵局仍有待观察。

Document 488: 【鑫期汇】宏观专题：民主党赢得两院：四条资产线（科技股、黄金、原油和铜）的四种定价-传导差异、短期方向与观察点

Source PDF: all report pdf/【鑫期汇】宏观专题：民主党赢得两院：四条资产线（科技股、黄金、原油和铜）的四种定价-传导差异、短期方向与观察点.pdf
Report source: 华鑫期货
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 6
Status: ok

### Page 1

【鑫期汇】宏观专题：民主党赢得两院：四条资产线（科技股
、黄金、原油和铜）的四种定价 
传导差异、短期方向与观察点 
2026 年 11 月 4 日 | 美股截至 11 月 3 日收盘；商品与股指期货为 11 月 4 日 13:36 | 华鑫期货研究所 
结果口径：本文以 NBC（11 月 4 日 03:08 UTC）、NPR 等媒体判定民主党赢得众议院与参议院（执政党中期选举落败）为前提展开，选举结果以
官方认证为准，属情景分析，非结果追述。 
核心判断 
01  首发反应与直觉相反。11 月 3 日纳指涨 0.83%，是结果出炉之前的收盘；结果明朗后，纳指期货 −0.54%、美
元 −0.35%、黄金 +1.30%、伦铜 −0.51%、美铜 −0.96%。真正的定价在结果之后。 
02  制度约束决定幅度。参院立法需 60 票，加税与能源立法难落地；232、301 属行政权，存量关税不会因国会易
手自动撤销。风险溢价先动、政策后动，时间差就是空间。 
03  四个品种不是同一个故事。科技股的主变量是资本开支回报率，选举只改变尾部风险；铜有独立利空，即关税
溢价剥离；黄金受 12 月加息压制；原油几乎不反应。 
04  美元共同项给出判别。美元走弱而金涨铜跌，说明铜的下跌有独立驱动，不是汇率造成的。 
05  短期方向已可排序（见第七节）。金银偏多；铜锡偏空，但跌幅已提前实现，空间收窄；原油偏空，跌幅尚未
实现。已实现读数与后续判断须分开看。

一  两个时点：结果前后的方向翻转 
11 月 3 日美股收盘，纳斯达克涨 0.83%、标普500 涨 0.36%、道琼斯跌 0.38%，此时结果尚未明朗。结果
公布后的亚洲时段（11 月 4 日 13:36），三大股指期货全部转跌：纳指期货 −0.54%、标普期货 −0.61%、道指
期货 −0.25%。 
商品与汇率同时给出三个方向。贵金属最强：COMEX 黄金涨 1.30%、白银涨 1.60%。能源几乎不动：WTI 
涨 0.17%、布伦特涨 0.30%。工业金属走弱：伦铜 −0.51%、美铜 −0.96%、伦锡 −0.40%。美元指数跌 0.35% 
至 101.51。 
若只看 11 月 3 日的涨幅，会得出“市场欢迎民主党”的结论，这是时点错配——那一天的上涨发生在结果出
炉前。结果明朗后的四个方向，才是对事件本身的定价。 
表 1 三大股指：结果前后的方向翻转 
品种 11/3 收盘 涨跌 11/4 13:36（期货） 涨跌 
纳斯达克 25,372.01 +0.83% 28,153.6 −0.54% 
标普500 7,568.92 +0.36% 7,550.8 −0.61% 
道琼斯 51,786.72 −0.38% 51,763.4 −0.25% 
数据来源：新浪财经；左侧为指数现货收盘，右侧为对应股指期货，涨跌对前结算价 
二  制度约束：三条路径与一道 60 票门槛 
民主党同时掌握两院，打破了过去两年白宫与两院同属共和党的格局。影响走三条路径，但每条都受同一
道门槛约束。 
财政。加税风险上升、财政扩张受限，长端利率与财政溢价有下行空间。但参议院立法实际需 60 票，加
税未必落地。 
监管。反垄断、人工智能监管、环保趋严，行政层面可较快推进，立法层面较慢。 
贸易。关税进一步升级的概率下降。但 232、301 均属行政权，国会不会因易手而自动撤销存量关税。

### Page 2

市场常把“民主党赢”直接映射为“加税必然、监管必然”，制度上并不成立。风险溢价先动、政策后动，这
个时间差才是交易空间所在。
三  科技股：选举是次要变量，不是主线
期货层面，纳指 −0.54%、标普 −0.61%、道指 −0.25%，科技小幅跑输，但幅度不足一个百分点，属温和
承压。
对照更有说服力。纳指自 10 月 28 日累跌 5.22%，其中包含科技巨头财报后的重挫；而本次结果明朗后的
期货跌幅不足 1%。量级差异说明，市场并未把选举当作科技股的主要矛盾。
当前主线是人工智能资本开支的回报率，不是税率，也不是反垄断。选举改变的是尾部风险（监管极端情
形），不改变主线。因此短期可能温和承压，也可能因政策不确定性下降而快速修复；中期的压制项是 12 月
加息与资本开支指引，与选举无关。
四  黄金：短期有美元支撑，趋势不看政治
COMEX 黄金 3,991.7 美元/盎司涨 1.30%，白银 50.55 美元/盎司涨 1.60%，与美元 −0.35% 同步，是典型
的“美元弱叠加避险”组合。

但内部逻辑其实相互抵消：加税预期推升避险需求（利多），财政扩张受限则压低财政风险溢价（利空）
。真正的压制项是 12 月加息——10 月 FOMC 以 10:2 维持 3.75%—4.00%，市场对 12 月至少加息 25BP 的定
价约 88%。金价处 3,990 美元高位，对加息最敏感。
所以选举给的是短期美元支撑，不是趋势级驱动。结构支撑仍是央行购金与实际利率，这一点事件没有改
变。
五  原油：反应最弱，反映的是当期无影响
WTI 涨 0.17%、布伦特涨 0.30%，是四类资产中反应最弱的。逻辑上民主党对化石能源不友好，供给端应
偏紧，实际几乎不动。
原因很直接：能源立法过不了 60 票门槛；行政层面（环保署规则、租赁许可）可以推进，但影响的是两
三年后的产量，当期供需不变。市场用近乎零的反应表达了“当期无影响”，这个定价是准确的。
六  铜：利空的品种
铜受影响最明确，且国内比海外更悲观：沪铜 2612 盘中 105,950 元/吨跌 1.19%，美铜跌 0.96%，伦铜
跌 0.51%，国内跌幅是伦铜的 2.35 倍。

路径一，关税溢价剥离。232 关税推进概率下降，此前“抢关税”导致的 COMEX 累库失去理由。COMEX 库
存 72.72 万吨、占全球显性库存 65.2%，这一流向的前提正是关税预期。价格上已有印证：COMEX 折美元/吨
13,979，伦铜 13,964.7，价差仅 +14.2 美元/吨，处公开研报给出的 0—200 美元区间下沿，囤铜叙事已失去价
格基础。
路径二，需求证伪。科技资本开支下修，数据中心用铜增量被打折。沪锡跌 3.81%，是沪铜的 3.19 倍；
而伦锡仅跌 0.40%，国内跌幅是伦锡的 9.63 倍。锡无现货缓冲，跌幅被放大；铜有缓冲，但缓冲正在变薄。
路径三，美元走弱。美元 −0.35%，对铜的支撑不足以对冲前两条。
持仓给出印证：沪铜全合约总持仓 586,330 手，较 11 月 3 日收盘增 23,571 手（+4.19%），价格下跌而
持仓增加，为增仓下跌。此处用总持仓而非主力合约口径，可剔除换月造成的假性增减。

### Page 3

一处必须克制的表述：232 属行政权，国会不能自动撤销存量关税。“库存回流”是方向而非既成事实，需
看到 COMEX 库存连续下降才能确认。

### Page 4

七  短期方向：金银偏多、铜锡跌幅有限、原油偏空
执政党在中期选举中落败。上文四节讲的是各方为何反应不同，本节给出短期方向，并先区分两件事：已
实现的价格反应，与后续的方向判断。前者是读数，后者是判断，混用会自相矛盾。
表 3 已实现反应与短期方向：四项对照
资产 已实现反应（13:36） 短期方向 主要依据 与读数关系
黄金／白银 金 +1.30%／银 +1.60% 偏多 美元 −0.35%，加税预期推升避险 一致
铜 沪铜 −1.19%；自 10/28 计
−2.98%
偏空，空间有限 价差已剥离至平水，利空提前反映 部分一致

原油 WTI +0.17%（对前结算）

偏空 制裁属行政权，缓和预期上升；需求走
